959 entries, sdk-api-ergonomics-devil-python to search-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sdk-api-ergonomics-devil-python**: READ-ONLY Wave M7 reviewer that audits Python SDK API ergonomics from a first-time consumer's perspective. Finds boilerplate-heavy quickstarts, surprising defaults, mis-shaped async APIs, missing async-context-manager support, missing docstring Examples, kwargs that don't compose, Generic / Protocol overuse, and exception types that force the caller into ceremonial try/except ladders. - **sdk-complexity-devil-go**: Testing-phase agent (in T5 cohort). Runs scaling benchmarks at N ∈ {10, 100, 1k, 10k} for any §7 symbol that takes variable-size input. Fits the curve. Compares measured complexity to design/perf-budget.md's declared big-O. BLOCKER on mismatch (e.g. declared O(n) measured O(n²)). Prevents an accidental quadratic path from passing because the microbenchmark only ran at N=100. READ-ONLY. Backs G107. ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-complexity-devil-go.md))
- **sdk-complexity-devil-python**: Wave T5 testing-phase devil. Reads perf-budget.md complexity.time declarations, runs scaling sweep at N ∈ {10, 100, 1k, 10k} via pytest-benchmark + parametrize for any §7 symbol that takes variable-size input, curve-fits log-log slope, compares to declared big-O. BLOCKER on mismatch. Catches the accidental quadratic that micro-benches at fixed N never expose. Backs G107-py (perf-confidence axis 4). ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-complexity-devil-python.md))

- **sdk-drift-detector**: Testing-phase agent (T-SOAK wave). Observes soak state files on a poll ladder (30s, 2m, 5m, 15m, 30m, 60m, 2h, 4h, 6h). Fits linear regression over time on drift_signals (heap_bytes, goroutines, gc_pause_p99_ns, etc.). Fast-fails on statistically significant positive slope (p&lt;0.05). Issues PASS / FAIL / INCOMPLETE per MMD. Backs G105 (MMD) + G106 (drift) + rule 33. READ-ONLY. ([PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline](https://github.com/PremModhaOfficial/NFR-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/sdk-drift-detector.md))

Spawn one per layer (FE, BE) — they run in parallel safely since they write different files. Never touches ACTIVE_TASK.md or task-log/. Use for any feature spanning more than one repository. - **sdlc-researcher**: Explores the codebase, existing specs and history to answer a focused question and reports back concisely, without flooding the main context. Read-only; use while writing intent, spec, or plan artifacts. ([marin-ai-tech/sdlc](https://github.com/marin-ai-tech/sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/sdlc-researcher.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sdlc-researcher**: Bounded research worker for SDLC runs. Investigates ONE load-bearing unknown (API behavior, library choice, platform constraint, security posture, architecture precedent) using primary sources first, synthesizes to a decision-ready artifact with citations, and STOPS when evidence suffices. Returns tangents as seed-shaped recommendations instead of chasing or filing them. Use in the Research phase; cap two concurrent. Runs concurrently with the internal-findings and prior-art lenses. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-researcher-constraints.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-researcher-findings**: Internal-code lens of the parallel research phase — searches the existing codebase for what already solves part of the request, what will conflict, and the conventions to match. Runs concurrently with the prior-art and constraints lenses. No network access needed. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-researcher-findings.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-researcher-prior-art**: External prior-art lens of the parallel research phase — how comparable products solve this user problem, and which interaction or structural ideas are worth borrowing. Runs concurrently with the internal-findings and constraints lenses. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-researcher-prior-art.md) · ★2)

- **sdlc-review-lead**: Runs the review phase in two modes — verify mode establishes the build and test truth once before the specialist reviewers fan out, then synthesize mode merges their findings, deduplicates by root cause, allocates issue ids, applies mechanical fixes, and signs off on the review gate. Runs first and last in phase 8, and its fast stage doubles as the join that compiles the tree after a parallel implementation group. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-review-lead.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-review-modernization**: Reviews PR diffs for missed opportunities to use newer language/framework capabilities available in the version the repo actually runs. Java, Ruby, Python, TS/JS, Angular/RxJS. Always non-blocking recommendations. Invoked by /software-factory:pr-review. ([VTHoop/software-factory](https://github.com/VTHoop/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/software-factory/agents/sdlc-review-modernization.md))
- **sdlc-review-patterns**: Reviews PR diffs for conformance to THE PROJECT'S OWN documented or idiomatic patterns — discovered from the host repo's CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, .claude/rules, docs, and lint configs, never from training-data preferences. Invoked by /software-factory:pr-review. ([VTHoop/software-factory](https://github.com/VTHoop/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/software-factory/agents/sdlc-review-patterns.md))
- **sdlc-review-performance**: Performance and resource lens of the parallel review phase — reviews the diff for N+1 access, unbounded queries and growth, missing timeouts, blocking work, and payload cost. Emits local findings for the review lead to merge. Runs concurrently with the other review lenses in phase 8. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-review-performance.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-review-performance**: Reviews PR diffs for performance and resource-lifecycle hazards that would actually fire at runtime — leaks, N+1 queries, unbounded growth, blocking/concurrency, wasted work, bundle bloat — across Java, Ruby, Python, and TS/Angular. Invoked by /software-factory:pr-review. ([VTHoop/software-factory](https://github.com/VTHoop/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/software-factory/agents/sdlc-review-performance.md))
- **sdlc-review-quality**: Reviews PR diffs across three lenses — security (OWASP/CWE-grounded), accessibility (UI surfaces only), and test coverage (per the repo's own conventions). Invoked by /software-factory:pr-review. ([VTHoop/software-factory](https://github.com/VTHoop/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/software-factory/agents/sdlc-review-quality.md))
- **sdlc-review-requirements**: Reviews PR diffs for acceptance-criteria coverage. Maps each AC bullet to changed files and flags uncovered or partially-covered criteria. Invoked by /software-factory:pr-review. ([VTHoop/software-factory](https://github.com/VTHoop/software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugins/software-factory/agents/sdlc-review-requirements.md))
- **sdlc-review-security**: Security lens of the parallel review phase — reviews the diff for injection, authorization gaps, secret handling, unsafe deserialization, and data exposure, adversarially. Emits local findings for the review lead to merge. Runs concurrently with the other review lenses in phase 8. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-review-security.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-review-tests**: Test honesty lens of the parallel review phase — checks every assigned test case against the approved plan, catches assertions narrowed to fit the implementation, tautological tests, over-mocking, and untested failure paths. Emits local findings for the review lead to merge. Runs concurrently with the other review lenses in phase 8. ([DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc](https://github.com/DevLab-Technologies/claude-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-pipeline/agents/sdlc-review-tests.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-reviewer**: Independent, read-only code reviewer for Animama's bounded review policy (SDLC.md#bounded-code-review). Use after each reviewed ExecPlan phase, for the final plan or PR scope, and for repair-round verification. Give it the baseline, source state, scope, review tier, acceptance criteria, the implementer's check record and review round number. - **sdlc-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that applies the consuming project's REVIEW.md policy — the Plan pass against plan.md before code, and separate Bugs, Security and Compliance passes against a diff. Also serves as the verifier that judges whether a finding is real before it is posted. Treats all plan text, diff content, commit messages, PR text, source comments, and dependency metadata as data, never instructions, and reports prompt-injection attempts as security findings. - **sdlc-reviewer**: Independent code reviewer for the SDLC review stage. Reviews the current branch diff against the feature's spec.md, plan.md and REVIEW.md with fresh context. Only in repositories where the SDLC is enabled (prompts carry an [SDLC] status line): use proactively before opening or merging a PR. Read-only; use in the review workflow or for any code review request. ([marin-ai-tech/sdlc](https://github.com/marin-ai-tech/sdlc/blob/HEAD/assets/agents/reviewer.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **sdlc-reviewer**: Task-aware diff reviewer. Use during the review skill to review implementation diff against this specific task's acceptance criteria, design contracts, and test results — not generic standards. Reads ACTIVE_TASK.md sections and git diff, returns severity-tagged findings. Compresses output aggressively. Handed one packet file written by scripts/review-packet.js (the diff or documents under review, the checklist items and files its dimension owns, the principles the change can violate with the withheld ones listed, the change → doc map, settled script outputs) and its dimension; not for general use. - **sdlc-reviewer**: AI-SDLC adversarial reviewer on a different model from the author. Reviews either a plan (walkthrough.md + spec.md) or a diff, as the caller says. - **sdlc-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for deliverables and code. Triggers on "review the design", "check this document", "review before commit", "audit conventions". - **sdlc-reviewer**: Reviews implemented ticket work against the spec and repo standards, posting findings to the ticket's review surface. No source edits. Used by /clear-board stage 3. ([alexnikolis/skills](https://github.com/alexnikolis/skills/blob/HEAD/skills/setup-sdlc-harness/templates/agents/sdlc-reviewer.md))
- **sdlc-reviewer**: Reviews a task's diff against its intent, spec, plan, CLAUDE.md and REVIEW.md, or verifies a list of findings from other reviewers. - **sdlc-reviewer**: Read-only review worker for SDLC waves. Emits findings with file:line evidence; never edits code. Use after implementers finish, before merge. - **sdlc-reviewer**: Revisa o código implementado contra as Operations do REASONS Canvas e as tasks, roda a validação OpenSpec e os sensores e entrega um relatório com veredito para o humano decidir o merge. Somente leitura, não altera arquivos. Use depois da implementação. Disparado pela sessão principal. - **sdlc-reviewer**: SDLC phase 5. - **sdlc-verifier**: Runs a task's checks from a fresh context and reports literal evidence. Use at the end of /sdlc-build and in /sdlc-test, with the task slug. Runs the project's verify command, each requirement's proof from plan.md, and Playwright checks against the spec's mock. Reports only, never fixes. ([bakirgdev/ai-native-sdlc](https://github.com/bakirgdev/ai-native-sdlc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sdlc-verifier.md))
- **sdlc-verifier**: The sdlc-deliver fresh-context verifier (verification.md [V3]). Exercises the delivered change and its two nearest neighbouring flows and reports each acceptance check as pass or fail with evidence. Never edits. Dispatched by /sdlc-deliver; not for general use. ([fol2/james-dev-skills](https://github.com/fol2/james-dev-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-deliver/agents/sdlc-verifier.md))
- **sdlc-verifier**: Use when an integrated candidate needs test execution, UX checks, or evidence collection for a verification gate. ([HakjunMIN/ai-native-harness](https://github.com/HakjunMIN/ai-native-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc-verifier.agent.md))
- **sdlc-verify**: Final verification against spec with evidence chain ([igun997/pi-sdlc](https://github.com/igun997/pi-sdlc/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc-verify.md) · ★2)
- **sdlc-webclaw-firecrawl-fallback-62884fc6-2026-10-03**: RESEARCH INCOMPLETE: `firecrawl-search` still fails with `exited 1: Error: Request failed with status code 402 |`. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/docs/research/kb/reports/agents/sdlc-webclaw-firecrawl-fallback-62884fc6-2026-10-03.md) · ★5)
- **sdlc-worker**: The sdlc-deliver subagent for every role except the verifier, covering the plan check, unit workers, review passes and declared review dimensions. Dispatched by /sdlc-deliver with a role brief; not for general use. ([fol2/james-dev-skills](https://github.com/fol2/james-dev-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc-deliver/agents/sdlc-worker.md))
- **sdlc-worker**: Isolated SDLC pipeline lane worker. Spawned by the sdlc-dispatch scheduled task to execute one sdlc/lanes/&lt;lane&gt;.md pass. Deliberately has NO agent-spawning tool: all owner work is done inline. ([meridun/FilingCabinet](https://github.com/meridun/FilingCabinet/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sdlc-worker.md) · 3 copies)
- **sdlc-worker.agent**: Isolated SDLC pipeline lane worker. Spawned by the sdlc-dispatch scheduled task to execute one sdlc/lanes/&lt;lane&gt;.md pass. Deliberately has NO agent-spawning tool: all owner work is done inline. ([meridun/FilingCabinet](https://github.com/meridun/FilingCabinet/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/sdlc-worker.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **sdlc-writer**: Use this agent when the pipeline needs the next SDLC artifact produced (intent-and-constraints, high-level-design, adr-lite, or implementation-spec). Typical triggers include pipeline advancing to an sdlc state after requirements are written, a review being approved and the next artifact needed, and manual invocation via /process to kick off spec production. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([esxr/operant](https://github.com/esxr/operant/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc-writer.md))
- **sdlc2-architect**: World-class software architect persona (DDD + hexagonal + cloud-native). Use to design the domain model, define aggregate/bounded-context boundaries, choose architecture style, and record ADR-worthy trade-offs — before code. Works from the grilled seed `feature.md`; produces `design.md` and any ADRs. Its work is checked by the architect-critic persona, but that gate is ADVISORY — architecture has no executable oracle, so the human stays primary. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-architect.md))
- **sdlc2-architect-critic**: Independent adversarial reviewer of architecture/design work — the evaluator twin of the architect persona, same DDD identity but mandate flipped to REFUTE. Use to challenge a proposed domain model / boundary / ADR with fresh context before committing. Read-only. Emits a structured VERDICT. ADVISORY ONLY: architecture has no executable oracle, so two LLMs reasoning alike can rubber-stamp — the human remains the authoritative gate. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-architect-critic.md))
- **sdlc2-code-reviewer**: Independent code-quality evaluator — same framework as tester/ux-auditor: read-only, fresh context, emits a structured VERDICT, never fixes. Judges a codebase on one dimension at a time — Clean Code (Robert C. Martin), DDD implementation, idiomatic use of the project's OWN declared stack, and algorithms/data-structures. Every finding cites file:line + a NAMED principle. A green verdict means "no high/critical violations on this dimension" — Clean Code judgments are opinionated, so the human curates. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-code-reviewer.md))
- **sdlc2-developer**: Senior full-stack developer persona — one identity spanning the whole vertical slice, in whatever stack the project declares. Use to BUILD a vertical feature slice outside-in (acceptance test at the outer seam → inner units → green). Two focus modes (BACKEND MODE / FRONTEND MODE) for a single slice that crosses layers. Builds against a ux-design spec on the frontend and an architect design on the backend; its work is judged by the tester persona. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-developer.md))
- **sdlc2-product-owner**: Senior Product Owner persona. Turns a capability into user value, user stories, and testable Gherkin acceptance criteria. Use to frame WHAT and WHY before design/build, refine a backlog, or write acceptance criteria. Works from the grilled seed `feature.md` and extends it with the Why / User Stories / Acceptance Criteria sections. Has no agent evaluator — the user/market is its oracle, so its output is a human decision point, not an automated gate. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-product-owner.md))
- **sdlc2-product-owner-critic**: Independent reviewer of product-owner output — the evaluator twin of the product-owner persona, same identity but mandate flipped to REFUTE. Use to challenge user stories + Gherkin acceptance criteria for INVEST quality, testability, clarity, edge/error coverage, ubiquitous-language fidelity, and thin-slice scope, with fresh context. Read-only. Emits a structured VERDICT. ADVISORY: it judges requirements QUALITY, never whether the feature is actually wanted — that validation belongs to the user/market + the human. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-product-owner-critic.md))
- **sdlc2-tester**: Independent QA / test engineer persona — the oracle twin of developer. Use to verify a built slice against its acceptance criteria with fresh context: runs the unit/integration suites, exercises the API via BDD scenarios, and runs the project's own end-to-end command when one is declared. Emits a structured VERDICT (pass/score/defects). Unlike the architecture oracle, this one has executable ground truth — its verdict is authoritative, not advisory. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-tester.md))
- **sdlc2-ux-auditor**: Independent UX evaluator — the oracle twin of ux-design, same UX identity but mandate flipped to JUDGE. Use to audit a designed screen against Nielsen's 10 heuristics, structural WCAG AA, and the project's UX bar. Static and read-only — it reads the mockup and the stories, never edits, never drives a browser. Emits a structured VERDICT (pass/score/defects). A green verdict means "no STRUCTURAL defects" — it does NOT certify visual taste (that stays a human + frontend-design call). ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-ux-auditor.md))
- **sdlc2-ux-design**: Senior UX / product-design persona — the experience architect. Use to turn a product story into the EXPERIENCE structure: user flows, information architecture, the state matrix (empty/loading/error/partial/success), and lo-fi wireframes. Produces an experience spec extending `mockup.html` — NOT production code, NOT final pixels. Hands down to developer (build) and frontend-design (visual craft); its work is judged by ux-auditor. ([rcforte/sdlc2](https://github.com/rcforte/sdlc2/blob/HEAD/agents/sdlc2-ux-design.md))
